| 2026-05-26 | Krit | **SEPOS-BATCH-001 — Kitchen batch prep + expiry with chef tick-off (commit `0701615`).** Thai-kitchen inventory addition: chef defines batch recipes (curry pastes, stocks, sauces, marinated meats), system tracks per-batch expiry + locked cost, and menu recipes consume the batch like any other ingredient. **Schema (PG only — inventory is Pro plan, runs cloud-direct):** new `batch_recipes` (id, name, output_quantity, output_unit, shelf_life_days, total_cost, cost_per_unit, notes), `batch_recipe_lines` (FK + ingredient + qty + unit + line_cost), `batches` (made_on, expires_on, original_quantity, locked_cost_per_unit, status active/expired/discarded/used_up, discarded_qty/at/by, extended_count). `ingredients` gains `is_batch BOOLEAN + batch_recipe_id` so a batch row IS an ingredient — menu recipes pick it up automatically. **Backend:** CRUD `/api/batch-recipes` (POST auto-creates matching `ingredients` row; DELETE rejects 409 if any menu recipe line references it). `POST /api/batches/make` is the atomic make transaction — deducts every raw line, writes a `batch_prep` stock_movements row per line, computes total_cost from current ingredient prices (locks cost at make-time so paste cost stays stable when ingredient prices move next week), increments batch ingredient `current_stock` by output_quantity + snaps its `cost_per_unit`, inserts the batches row with `expires_on = CURRENT_DATE + shelf_life_days`. `POST /api/batches/:id/discard` — body `{quantity, reason}` default qty = original; subtracts from stock (clamped ≥0), writes 'waste' stock_movement with `cost_at_time = locked_cost_per_unit` so wastage report sees £ loss, status='discarded'. `POST /api/batches/:id/extend` — `expires_on +1 day`, status='active', `extended_count++`, capped at 3 extensions. `GET /api/batches` auto-flips `active→expired` on read so UI is current without a cron. **Frontend** — new "🥣 Batches" tab between Recipes and Stock. Two-pane: LEFT active batches with days-left countdown (≤1 day = amber border + "expires today/tomorrow"; expired = red border + Discard / "Still good (+1 day)" tick-off buttons); RIGHT batch recipes + "+ New recipe" + "🥣 Make" per row. MakeBatchModal previews exactly what'll happen before commit. DiscardModal asks "how much was left?" + reason + live wastage £. BatchRecipeModal filters ingredient picker to raw-only (no batch-from-batch — prevents cost loops). RecipesTab ingredient picker shows 🥣 prefix on batch ingredients. CSV export. **v1 limitation noted:** no per-batch FIFO at sale time. Total stock tracked at ingredient level; batches table is parallel tracking for expiry + discard management. v2 if a restaurant needs strict FIFO accounting. **Schema migrates on boot — Korakot to redeploy backend.** **Handoff to Nook:** create batch recipe (e.g. Red Curry Paste 2kg from 4 raw) → tap Make → verify raw stock deducted + batch stock +2kg + cost locked → add batch ingredient to a menu recipe → sell a dish → verify ingredient stock decremented → wait for expiry (or temporarily set shelf_life=0) → verify status auto-flipped → tick Discard with partial qty → verify wastage report shows £ loss → tick "Still good +1 day" on another expired → verify expires_on bumped + status back to active. | SEPOS-BATCH-001 |
| 2026-05-25 | Sam | **Online booking correctness batch — Stripe-less graceful degradation + past-date / past-slot blocking + date-first widget reorder + no-cache widget headers (commits `5193620`, `8cde26f`, `1974d71`, `cbbbe67`, `1e6ccf4`).** Five-commit batch fixing real correctness gaps Korakot surfaced while testing the live spa widget. **(1) `5193620` Stripe-less graceful degradation.** Korakot reported online bookings bouncing with "payment_intent_id required (deposit due)" even though the widget UI never showed a payment step. Root cause: client/server disagreement — widget correctly checks `/stripe-config` and skips Stripe when `configured: false`, but server's `/book` was strict (deposit_model != 'none' AND amount > 0 = must have payment_intent_id regardless). Fix: gate the deposit-enforcement block on `stripeClient()` being non-null. If Stripe isn't configured we silently skip the deposit check, log a warning, and let the booking through with `deposit_amount=0`. Operator can still capture deposits via SPA-DEPOSIT-MANUAL once the booking lands. **(2) `8cde26f` past-date blocking + date-first reorder.** Two bugs in one ship. **Past dates:** `POST /api/widget/book` rejects `starts_at < now` with 400 "cannot book in the past"; `POST /api/appointments` (admin) rejects too, with `?allow_past=1` opt-out for recording a historical booking. Admin `NewAppointmentModal` date input gains `min=today` on CREATE (omitted on EDIT so a 14:00 booking can still be shuffled to 13:30 within today). **Date-first reorder (widget):** still 6 steps but combined Step 2 = Date+Therapist. Date picker on top with `min=today`, therapist list below filtered to whoever's on shift via new `GET /api/widget/therapists?date=YYYY-MM-DD` (reuses `getTherapistWorkingWindow` from availability service). Step 3 becomes Time-only with a "Saturday 25 May · with Anchara" header. If date changes and the picked therapist is off, selection auto-resets to "Any available". **(3) `1974d71` no-cache headers on widget.js.** After ship #2, Korakot reported the new widget wasn't visible — curl confirmed Railway WAS serving the new file but his browser cached the old one. Added `Cache-Control: no-cache, must-revalidate` to `/widget.js` + `/booking-widget.js`. Express auto-sets an ETag so unchanged widgets return 304 (~0 bytes, ~50ms) — only changed widgets ship the full ~30KB. After this commit, all future redeploys propagate on next page load instead of waiting hours for cache TTL. **(4) `cbbbe67` belt-and-braces past-date guard for mobile pickers.** HTML5 `<input type="date">` min attribute isn't strictly enforced on every browser (Safari iOS notorious for scrolling past min). Three new layers on top of the existing `min=today`: (a) `onChange` handler that snaps `state.date` back to today + shows clear error if user picks a past date; (b) amber warning banner under the input when `state.date < today`; (c) Continue button hard-disabled when past + re-validates on click. Server-side rejection from `8cde26f` already in place as final safety net. **(5) `1e6ccf4` past time-slot filter on widget /availability.** Korakot's actual complaint after #4: not past DATES but past TIME slots for today. At 14:00 the widget was still showing 10:00 slots. `computeAvailability` generates slots from opening_time onward without comparing to now. Fix at the widget endpoint only (admin keeps full slot list for retroactive walk-in entries): filter out any slot starting before `now + 30 min`. The 30-min lead-time buffer prevents "book a slot starting in 2 minutes" which would leave no room prep time. **Test plan for Nook:** open widget → step 2 shows date picker on top + therapist list below filtered by rota · pick yesterday → blocked, amber banner appears · pick today + therapist who's off today → therapist tile doesn't appear · pick today + Anchara at 14:00 → step 3 shows only slots ≥ 14:30 · curl `POST /api/widget/book` with past `starts_at` → 400 "cannot book in the past" · curl with no `STRIPE_SECRET_KEY` env → booking goes through with `deposit_amount=0`. | SPA-WIDGET-FIXES |
| 2026-05-25 | Sam | **SPA-DEPOSIT-{BADGE,MANUAL,DAILY} — deposit visibility across timeline + modal + summary card (commit `f207917`).** Korakot's ask: the team should be able to see at a glance which bookings have a deposit on file, and the receptionist needs a way to record a deposit taken by cash or card terminal (not just Stripe). Three small features built together because they share the same `appointments.deposit_*` columns. **(1) SPA-DEPOSIT-BADGE — 💳 corner badge on timeline blocks.** Active bookings with `deposit_amount > 0` show a green corner badge (`💳 £25`) in the top-right of the appointment block on TimelineView. Pending Stripe links show amber. Title attribute carries exact amount + method on hover. Hides automatically once `status='completed'` so the bottom-right payment-method badge isn't crowded. Legend row gains "💳 £ — Deposit on file". Receptionists see what's already collected without opening anything. **(2) SPA-DEPOSIT-MANUAL — Take deposit at till (cash / card terminal).** Three new schema columns: `deposit_method` (cash / card), `deposit_taken_at` (timestamp), `deposit_taken_by` (therapist FK). New backend endpoints — `POST /api/appointments/:id/deposit-manual { amount, method }` refuses if a Stripe deposit already exists, refuses if the bill is closed, otherwise UPSERTs deposit_amount + method + sets `payment_status='deposit_paid'`. `DELETE /:id/deposit-manual` clears (operator mistake). Both emit `appointment_updated` socket. UI: new `ManualDepositBlock` in `NewAppointmentModal` — amber "💵 Take deposit at till" panel with £ amount input + quick-fill buttons (policy default £25 + full treatment price) + two payment buttons (💵 Cash / 💳 Card). Existing manual deposit renders a green "💵 £X CASH/CARD deposit taken HH:MM" line with a Clear button. Block hides if a Stripe deposit exists (operator can't overwrite online) or if the bill is closed. Confirm dialog before recording. The existing bill auto-credit logic in `bills.js` already reads `appointment.deposit_amount`, so manual deposits flow through transparently — operator collects only the balance at checkout. **(3) SPA-DEPOSIT-DAILY — Daily summary card on AppointmentScreen.** Cream/gold banner above the timeline showing: `💳 Today · 4/12 with deposit · £100.00 collected · 🌐 £50 online · 💵 £25 cash · 💳 £25 card · ⏳ 1 pending link`. Computed client-side from the appointments list already in state — no extra API call, auto-refreshes on socket events. Mobile collapses to count + total only. Backend also exposes `GET /api/appointments/deposit-summary?date=…` for any future external dashboards. **Schema migrates on boot** — Korakot to redeploy backend. **Test plan for Nook:** (a) create booking via admin → ManualDepositBlock visible → record £25 cash → badge appears on timeline · (b) open the booking → "💵 £25 CASH deposit taken" line → Clear → badge disappears · (c) generate Stripe deposit link → ManualDepositBlock hides (correct — can't overwrite online) · (d) close bill at checkout → bill total subtracts deposit · (e) refresh AppointmentScreen → daily card shows correct breakdown. | SPA-DEPOSIT-BADGE, SPA-DEPOSIT-MANUAL, SPA-DEPOSIT-DAILY |
| 2026-05-25 | Sam | **SPA-TREATMENT-VISIBILITY + duration-first booking + Treatwell one-tap + payment confirm dialog.** Four small Korakot-requested polish ships. **(1) SPA-TREATMENT-VISIBILITY (commit `bbd4805` — part 1).** Spa owners don't want every menu item on the public widget — VIP packages, long rituals, treatments under trial, etc. should be in-store/phone only. New `treatments.online_bookable BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE` column (defaults TRUE so nothing changes for live spas). `GET /api/widget/treatments` filters `active = TRUE AND online_bookable = TRUE`; admin `/treatments` still returns everything plus the flag. POST + PUT accept `online_bookable` (COALESCE with TRUE so omitted body fields don't accidentally hide a treatment). TreatmentMenuSection Edit modal gains a green/amber checkbox panel "🌐 Show on online booking widget"; list rows show a yellow "🚫 in-store only" pill on the treatment name when the flag is off. **(2) Duration-first booking (commit `bbd4805` — part 2).** When a spa has many treatments at similar lengths, picking the right one is faster if you narrow by duration first. NewAppointmentModal gains a "Duration" row of pill buttons above the treatment select — `[All] [30 min] [45 min] [60 min] [90 min] …` built dynamically from the unique durations in the treatment list. Default `'all'`. Picking a duration narrows the treatment dropdown; picking a treatment directly auto-syncs the filter to that treatment's duration so the UI stays coherent. Empty-state helper appears when a filter has no matches. **(3) Treatwell button — one-tap for full prepay (commit `41377c8`).** Korakot reported: "for Treatwell prepay, customer already paid full amount but I can't put £0 on payment". The TreatwellPaymentPanel forced an amount input; for full-prepay bookings that's pointless (the answer is always the bill total) and the mental model "I collected £0" didn't fit the panel's "what Treatwell paid" prompt. Fix: branch on `appt.treatwell_payment_type` — `'partial'` → open the panel, anything else → one tap closes the bill as `method='treatwell'` (bill total stays at full price = what Treatwell settles minus commission; till takes £0). Tooltip adapts. **(4) Payment confirm dialog (commit `3a3cb23`).** New `payConfirm(method, label)` wrapper in CheckoutScreen — Cash / Card / Treatwell-full-prepay now `confirm()` before closing. Messages: "Confirm: take £30 by CASH and close this bill?", "Confirm: take £30 by CARD and close this bill?", "Confirm: close this bill as paid by TREATWELL? (Till takes £0; Treatwell settles to your account)". Split modal, Voucher panel, Treatwell-partial panel already have their own inline confirmation — skipped to avoid double confirm. Voucher legacy no-code button already had its own confirm() (existed before this change). | SPA-TREATMENT-VISIBILITY, SPA-DURATION-FILTER, SPA-TREATWELL-1TAP, SPA-PAY-CONFIRM |

Treatwell + admin paths do a small follow-up SELECT to attach therapist + client + treatment names since INSERT only returns raw columns. | 2026-05-25 | Krit | **TableMapScreen blank-page bug — actual root cause found + fixed (commit `b395dd9`).** Same `.toFixed`-on-PG-string class as yesterday's TradingSection bug, but in `TableMapScreen.jsx` — when a user tapped an online/takeaway order, the tap-action popup tried to render `£{(tableActionPopup.order.total || 0).toFixed(2)}`. Because pg returns NUMERIC as string, `"24.10".toFixed(2)` raised TypeError, which propagated up and unmounted **the entire TableMapScreen** (no error boundary above it) → whole page blanked. **This was the real cause of Korakot's "click online order → bill blank" symptom** — the bill modal never even mounted because the popup that opens it crashed first. The previous backend 404 re-fix (`bc3f3cc`) was treating a different (also real) issue but didn't address the visible symptom. Verified the API path was healthy via curl: live Baan Siam order #4 returns full `{order, settings}` shape with `total:"14.70"` (string). Both unsafe sites in TableMapScreen wrapped in `Number()`. Three more latent OrderScreen sites (`item.price.toFixed`, `modifierPopup.item.price.toFixed`, `opt.extra_price.toFixed`) picked up by a concurrent session as commit `b4c774f`. **Methodology lesson:** when chasing a "blank UI" symptom, always trace what renders FIRST in the chain — the symptom showed at the bill modal but the crash was upstream in the popup that opens it. Should have curl'd the API + inspected the live bundle before assuming the bill component was at fault. | TABLEMAP-BLANK-FIX |
| 2026-05-25 | Krit | **Admin .toFixed-on-PG-string audit + bill 404 re-fix (commits `e5d724f`, `bc3f3cc`).** Yesterday's TradingSection fix (`4297dad`) revealed a class of latent bug: PostgreSQL `SUM/AVG NUMERIC` returns as **string** via the pg driver, and any `.toFixed()` called directly on it raises a TypeError that unmounts the admin React tree. (This is what actually caused the Baan Siam "flash then blank" symptom; the bill-modal fix I reverted last week was innocent.) Audited every admin section + inventory tab. **Patched** (Number() wrap at every API-money render site): ReportsSection (order.total, total_dine_in/takeaway/sales), BillsSection (b.total reduces — were string-concatenating! — plus bill.total + bill.paid_amount), ZReportSection (~10 sites: total_sales/cash/card/dine_in/takeaway/discounts/avg_per_cover + p.value in payment-methods row + r.total_sales in history list), CostSalesTab (totalRevenue coerced at source — all downstream arithmetic was already number-safe), MenuSection (dish.price on AI scanner result). Verified-safe sites left alone: locally-computed reduces already using Number() (totalSales/totalCash/totalCard), arithmetic results (avgPerHead/avgPerCover/expectedCash/billTotal/subtotal/serviceCharge/vatTotal), parseFloat outputs (actualNum/floatNum), per-staff seconds (totalHours). BillPeek already had a safe `money()` helper. **Bill fix re-applied backend-only (`bc3f3cc`):** `GET /api/orders/:id/bill` now returns `404 {error:'Order not found'}` when the order doesn't exist (was previously spreading `undefined` into `{order:{items:[]}}` which is truthy and rendered blank). No frontend changes this time — minimal diff, can't possibly touch the admin tree. Frontend's existing `.catch` on `getBill` will surface the 404 in the err state. **Lesson for next time:** when PG returns NUMERIC fields, treat them as strings on arrival. Either coerce at the API layer or wrap every `.toFixed` site in `Number()`. The latent bugs sit dormant until a tenant's first SUM is non-zero. | ADMIN-TOFIXED-AUDIT, BILL-404 |
| 2026-05-25 | Sandy (Cowork) | **SPA-003 Treatwell — 4 bugs fixed + E2E test suite 31/31 ✅.** Korakot reported bad feedback on Treatwell booking and checkout. Deep investigation found 4 bugs: **(BUG-TW-001 CRITICAL)** `treatwell_payment_type` defaulted to `'partial'` for ALL standard Treatwell marketplace bookings because the final `else` branch in `treatwell.js` set partial when the payload had no `payment_type` field — but Treatwell's standard marketplace booking never sends that field. Fix: final else now defaults to `'full'`. **(BUG-TW-003 HIGH)** Double-paying an already-paid bill returned HTTP 200 silently (the UPDATE WHERE payment_status != 'paid' returned 0 rows, bill row was null, but no check was done). Fix: added `AND payment_status != 'paid'` to the UPDATE, then distinguished 404 vs 409 with a second SELECT. **(BUG-TW-004 MEDIUM)** Deleting a bill left `appointments.payment_status = 'fully_paid'` — deposit looked consumed even though the bill was gone. Fix: DELETE /api/bills/:id now rolls `fully_paid → deposit_paid` in the UPDATE appointments. **(BUG-SPA-PAY-002 CRITICAL)** Split payment failed for any appointment with an online deposit — the split sum validation compared against `bills.total` (full amount) but the frontend sends the balance (total − deposit). Fix: validation now joins `appointments.deposit_amount` and validates against the balance. **(BUG-TW-002 UX)** Treatwell checkout banner re-appeared after payment was recorded. Fix: CheckoutScreen now shows a green "✓ Treatwell payment recorded" strip when `bill.payment_method === 'treatwell'` or `discount_reason` contains "Treatwell paid". **server.js** refactored: boot IIFE guarded with `require.main === module` + `module.exports = app` so test runner can require the app without double-booting. **Test suite:** `test-treatwell.js` (11 blocks, 31 checks) + `test-treatwell-runner.js` (pg-mem harness: registers TRIM/NULLIF/LENGTH/NOW, rewrites LATERAL→stripped, NOT IN→inequalities, bcrypt PINs). **Commit:** `6146991`. **Push needed** (sandbox has no GitHub creds). | SPA-003, BUG-TW-001/002/003/004, BUG-SPA-PAY-002 |
| 2026-05-25 | Sandy (Cowork) | **BUG-SPA-PAY-002 + BUG-SPA-DISCOUNT-001 — 4 payment bugs fixed.** Korakot reported bad feedback on spa checkout payments. Root-cause investigation found 4 bugs: **(1) BUG-SPA-PAY-002 (CRITICAL)**: Split payment failed with HTTP 400 "does not match bill total" for ANY appointment that had a Stripe online deposit. The first split validation in `POST /bills/:id/pay` queried `bills.total` but not `deposit_amount`, then compared the frontend's split sum (which sums to `balance = total − deposit`) against the full bill total — always a mismatch. Fix: joined `appointments` to get `deposit_amount` in the split validation query; introduced `expectedSum = billTotal − depositAmount`; removed the now-dead second validation block that was supposed to handle this but was never reachable. 2 edits to `spa-epos/src/routes/bills.js`. **(2) BUG-SPA-DISCOUNT-001 (HIGH)**: Discount % preset buttons called `saveDiscount(amount, "${p}% off")` which SET the discount absolutely — silently wiping any Treatwell partial or voucher partial payment previously recorded as a discount. Fix: introduced `applyPercentDiscount()` guard in `CheckoutScreen.jsx` that checks if `bill.discount_reason` contains "Treatwell paid" or "Voucher " and shows a `confirm()` warning before overwriting. **(3) BUG-SPA-DISCOUNT-002 (MEDIUM)**: `discountReason` local state started as `''` and was never initialised from `bill.discount_reason` — so changing the custom amount input after page load wiped the saved reason. Fix: initialise `discountReason` + `discountDraft` from the bill in the `load()` callback after `setBill`. **(4) BUG-SPA-DISCOUNT-003 (MEDIUM)**: Custom discount £ input fired `saveDiscount()` on every keystroke via `onChange` — two API calls when typing "15" (saves £1 then £15). Fix: replaced with `discountDraft` local state + `onBlur` that only calls `saveDiscount` when the value actually changed. All 4 fixes verified with `node --check` (bills.js) and inline assertion script (CheckoutScreen). | BUG-SPA-PAY-002, BUG-SPA-DISCOUNT-001 |

Added: (a) `NewAppointmentModal` — green banner at top in Edit mode when `deposit_amount > 0`, status-suffixed ("balance due at till" / "refunded" / "forfeit" / "bill closed"). (b) `ClientProfileScreen` — small green pill on each history row "🌐 Online · £25 deposit". (c) `BillsSection` — split bills now show the underlying breakdown inline under the method label (`🌐 deposit £25 / 💵 cash £45`). (d) `ZReportSection` + reports.js z-report — relabels the 'deposit' row as "🌐 deposit (online)" and adds a complete "Online deposits (Stripe)" block (taken / refunded / pending / consumed / forfeit). **(2) Z-Report export + per-bill detail (commit `6f2881a`).** Added 📥 Export CSV button + 🖨 Print button to the Z-Report screen. CSV is line-by-line: one row per bill with per-method columns (Cash / Card / Voucher / Deposit / Treatwell) auto-populated from `split_payments[]`, so an accountant can sum a column in Excel and read each method's total straight off. Summary footer includes totals + by-payment-method + online-deposit Stripe activity. BOM-prefixed UTF-8 so Excel renders £ correctly. New `@media print` block in styles.css strips nav/sidebar/buttons/modal backdrops, widens `.admin-layout`, keeps cards `page-break-inside-avoid`. Also added a bills-detail table to the Z-Report card (same data the CSV exports) so the on-screen view is scannable + the print copy carries the bills. **(3) SPA-PAY-002 — payment-link for phone/WhatsApp bookings (commit `46e80c9`).** Use case Korakot flagged: customer rings up wanting a specific therapist + treatment + time and wants to pay immediately without going through the website widget. Now: receptionist creates the booking in admin, taps "💳 Request deposit by link" on the appointment Edit modal (new `PaymentLinkBlock` component, shown when no deposit + policy != 'none'), backend `POST /api/appointments/:id/payment-link` creates a fresh Stripe PI + flips `payment_status='deposit_pending'` + returns the manage-link URL. Receptionist gets a Copy / 📱 WhatsApp (wa.me deep-link, prefilled text) / ✉️ Email (mailto with prefilled body) row. Customer taps link → opens `my-booking.html` which now detects `deposit_pending` and renders a Pay Now panel with Stripe Elements PaymentElement (tabs layout). After confirmCardPayment, frontend calls new public `POST /by-token/:token/confirm-payment` which re-verifies the PI with Stripe server-side before flipping to `deposit_paid`. The same URL serves Pay Now → Manage → Cancel-with-refund as the booking evolves. **(4) Z-Report shop identity + voucher sales (commit `32d11c0`).** Added: spa identity header (centred Georgia heading + address/phone/email line) on the Z-Report card / CSV first rows / print view — backend loads `settings.spa_name/spa_email/spa_address/spa_phone` with env-var (`SPA_NAME`/`SPA_EMAIL`/`SPA_ADDRESS`) fallback. Voucher sales section in z-report endpoint + screen + CSV summary (same shape the trading endpoint already returned, count / total / by_payment_method). **Files across all 4 commits:** 17 files, +~700/−~10. All E2E-verified locally. **Still requires Railway env vars** for SPA-PAY-002 to actually generate links: `STRIPE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY`, `STRIPE_SECRET_KEY`, `STRIPE_WEBHOOK_SECRET`, `BOOKING_SECRET` (same as SPA-PAY-001). Without them, the "Request deposit by link" button returns 503 cleanly; everything else works. | SPA-PAY-001, SPA-PAY-002 |

| 2026-05-22 | Sam | **SPA-PAY-001 — Online booking deposit (Stripe) + customer self-service amendments + Online Booking admin manager.** Full end-to-end online-deposit flow shipped in one bundle (commit `ff51c20`, 13 files, +1512). **(1) Widget Stripe deposit.** Lazy-loads Stripe.js, new step 5 "Pay deposit" between Details and Confirmation. `POST /api/widget/payment-intent` creates a Stripe PI for the quoted booking; appointment is NOT created until the PI verifies as `succeeded` server-side, so abandoned payments never leave phantom appointments. Reads `amount_received` from Stripe to defend against client tampering. Graceful fallback: if `STRIPE_SECRET_KEY`/`STRIPE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY` aren't set on Railway, the widget books without deposit so spas without Stripe configured still work. 4 deposit models supported: `none` / `fixed_amount` (default £25) / `percentage` / `full_prepay`. **(2) Customer self-service portal.** `client/public/my-booking.html` is a mobile-first static page served from `spa-api.siamepos.co.uk/my-booking.html?token=…` (HMAC-signed appointment-id token, separate `BOOKING_SECRET` from `UNSUB_SECRET`). No login. Reschedule (slot picker re-uses `/api/widget/availability`, server revalidates rota + availability before saving) and Cancel (inside policy window = Stripe refund issued + `payment_status='refunded'`; outside = forfeit, customer told why in the email). All amendments append to a new `appointment_amendments` audit table with `by_customer=TRUE`. **(3) Email templates.** `sendBookingConfirmation` extended with deposit / balance / "Manage your booking" gold CTA; new `sendBookingRescheduled` (old/new times with strike-through) and `sendBookingCancelled` (refund or forfeit explainer). Branded navy/gold layout matching the voucher gift email. **(4) Checkout deposit credit.** `CheckoutScreen` shows a green "💳 Deposit paid online −£25" line + gold "Balance due now £30" pill when `appointment.deposit_amount > 0`. Split modal opens with `balance` as the total to split. Backend `POST /api/bills/:id/pay` auto-credits the deposit: if a bill closes with a deposit on the appointment, the chosen method (cash/card) is converted to `'split'` and a `'deposit'` row is prepended to `split_payments`. Customer-built split payments are revalidated against the balance, not the full total. Stops bills showing £55-cash when the customer really paid £25 deposit + £30 cash. **(5) New 🌐 Online Booking admin tab** (`OnlineBookingSection.jsx`, mounted under Settings in `AdminScreen` NAV). Top: policy settings card with 4-button deposit-model picker, amount/percentage/cancel-window/policy-text fields (save on blur/click). Middle: stats strip (Upcoming online / Deposits held £ / Cancelled). Bottom: bookings list filtered to `source='online'` with client + therapist + status pill + Stripe-dashboard deep-link + Refund action. **(6) Reports.** `/api/reports/trading` adds an `online_deposits` block (count_pending / count_consumed / count_refunded / count_forfeit + total_taken / total_refunded). TradingSection renders "🌐 Online deposits — settled to Stripe, not the till" card so the owner doesn't double-count Stripe revenue against till cash. **DB migrations on first boot:** `appointments.deposit_amount NUMERIC(10,2)`, `deposit_stripe_id TEXT`, `payment_status TEXT` (none/deposit_paid/refunded/forfeit/fully_paid); new `appointment_amendments` table + index; 5 default settings keys (deposit_model='fixed_amount', deposit_amount='25', deposit_percentage='25', cancel_window_hours='24', cancel_policy_text=…) seeded via `ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING`. **E2E verified locally:** settings table loads keys cleanly; widget `/stripe-config` returns publishable_key+policy publicly; `/payment-intent` 503s cleanly without Stripe creds; bookingToken round-trips (GET `/api/booking/by-token/:token` returns booking + policy + editable flag; bogus tokens 401). **Required Railway env vars to enable real Stripe deposits:** `STRIPE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY`, `STRIPE_SECRET_KEY`, `STRIPE_WEBHOOK_SECRET`, `BOOKING_SECRET` (`openssl rand -hex 32`). Without them, widget falls back to no-deposit booking — safe. The admin tab works immediately for editing policy. **Stripe webhook URL to register:** `https://spa-api.siamepos.co.uk/api/stripe/webhook` (event `payment_intent.succeeded` — endpoint already wired). | SPA-PAY-001 |
| 2026-05-21 | Krit | **Baan Siam multi-tenancy bug — widget bookings invisible in admin.** Two compounding root causes. **(1) `RESTAURANT_ID` env var was unset on Baan Siam's Railway service** → backend defaulted to `'siamepos'` → widget POSTs correctly tagged bookings as `restaurant_id='baan-siam'` but the admin's `GET /api/reservations` filtered by env-default `'siamepos'` → invisible. Korakot set `RESTAURANT_ID=baan-siam` on Railway + ran the migration SQL (`UPDATE reservations/orders/order_items/payments/menu_items/categories/staff/tables/settings SET restaurant_id='baan-siam' WHERE restaurant_id='siamepos'`). **(2) `ReservationsScreen.jsx` + `ReservationPlanView.jsx` each inlined their own `getServerURL` that hardcoded `restaurant-epos-production`** — bypassing `VITE_API_URL` — so the Reservations tab on `app.baan-siam.siamepos.co.uk` (per-client Netlify deploy) silently queried the MAIN backend regardless of build env. Most other screens were fine (they import the canonical `SERVER_URL` from `api.js`). Fixed in **7f33266** — both screens now import from `'../api'`; `utils/serverDetect.js` cloud-probe URL also respects `VITE_API_URL`. **Prevention for future clients:** `scripts/provision-client.sh` hardened in **4f13cd9** — verifies `railway variables` shows `RESTAURANT_ID=<slug>` immediately after `variables set`, and post-deploy curls `/api/restaurant` to confirm the live deployment identifies as the right slug, with loud warn + remediation command if not. **(3) The deployed siamepos.net mockup site had a stale `SW_RID='siamepos'`** in its booking widget — fix was committed in `6b79bed` but never deployed because that Netlify site is **drag-and-drop, NOT git-connected** → `git push` doesn't propagate. So every new widget booking was born tagged `'siamepos'` on Baan Siam's DB and invisible to the `baan-siam`-filtered admin (the socket notification still fired globally, but the filtered re-fetch dropped the row — exactly the "notification but no row" symptom we hit). Korakot redeployed siamepos.net via drag-and-drop of `client/MockUp Website/` + re-ran the migration SQL one more time. End-to-end verified after. **Lessons for next time:** (a) When chasing a "data missing" bug across multiple deployments, **curl the LIVE asset and compare with the repo** — would have caught the SW_RID staleness in the first 5 minutes. (b) Migration SQL *camouflages* symptoms by relabeling old rows; it doesn't fix the source. Always make a NEW write after a fix to verify the loop is closed end-to-end (not just look at re-tagged existing rows). (c) **Hook siamepos.net to git** so future fixes auto-deploy — Netlify → Site settings → Build & deploy → Link site to Git, base directory `client/MockUp Website`. | BAAN-SIAM-FIX |

(8) ReportsSection adds a "Booking source — {date}" card with one tile per source (label / appointments / revenue). **Bonus fix:** patched a pre-existing PG syntax bug in `/reports/trading` — `COUNT(*)::int FILTER (...)` is invalid syntax; the cast must wrap the filtered aggregate. Found when my new ReportsSection fetched the trading endpoint for the first time. **E2E verified locally:** webhook with secret → appointment + client created (treatment matched) → re-send dedups → /api/clients returns the client with `acquisition_source: 'treatwell'` → cancellation flips status. **Scope cap:** full in-app Brevo campaign sending de-scoped — SiamSpa has no email-sending system yet, so owner exports filtered Treatwell customers as CSV and sends via their email tool. Brevo integration is a separate ticket. **Status:** implemented locally, needs commit + push + Railway boot (migrates `treatwell_booking_id` + index) + production env var `TREATWELL_WEBHOOK_SECRET` set + Treatwell portal registered the webhook URL `https://spa-api.siamepos.co.uk/api/treatwell/webhook`. | SPA-003, SPA-CRM-001 |

(2) `database.js` — `ALTER TABLE reservations ADD COLUMN marketing_consent/unsubscribed_at/table_ids` ran before reservations CREATE TABLE → moved after. (3) `server.js /api/setup/restaurant` — used non-existent `id` + `email` columns on restaurants table (PK is `restaurant_id` VARCHAR) → fixed. **Full provisioning checklist for future clients:** (1) Run `./scripts/provision-client.sh` (2) Manually create Railway service + Postgres in dashboard (3) Press Enter in script → it sets env vars + deploys (4) Wait for green deploy, then run 2 curl commands to seed owner login + restaurants row (5) In Namecheap add CNAME `{slug}` → Railway URL + TXT verify record (6) In Railway add custom domain `{slug}.siamepos.co.uk` (7) Add BREVO_API_KEY to Railway env vars (8) In Netlify create new site from same repo, set `VITE_API_URL=https://{slug}.siamepos.co.uk`, deploy (9) In Namecheap add CNAME `app.{slug}` → `siamepos-{slug}.netlify.app` (10) In Netlify add custom domain `app.{slug}.siamepos.co.uk`. Total ~20 min. **Known limitation:** provisioning script seeds login/restaurant row immediately after deploy trigger but service may still be booting → re-run curls manually if warnings appear. | SEPOS-CLIENT-001 |

| 2026-05-16 | Krit | All (esp. Nong / onboarding) | **⚠️ PRINTER SETUP GOTCHA — cnfujun / POS80 thermal printer.** Symptom: receipts print as raw HTML/code on paper while the on-screen preview looks fine. Cause: macOS auto-installs the printer with the **"Generic PostScript Printer"** driver — the thermal printer can't read PostScript so it prints the page code as text. FIX: install the cnfujun driver (cnfujun.com/d/33), then in System Settings → Printers & Scanners set the printer's driver/"Use" to the **POS-80** driver (NOT Generic PostScript, NOT Generic PCL). Verify with Terminal: `lpoptions -p <printer>` should show `printer-make-and-model=POS-80`. Every client installing this printer will hit this — make it a step in the onboarding checklist. |

## ✅ WEBSITE SERVICE PRICING — SETTLED, site updated (Korakot + Nick, 2026-07-20)

**Korakot's decision, final: the Website Service is £5/month. The £199 one-off comes OFF the public site — the build is quoted case by case.** £5/mo covers the website, hosting, the client's own domain (registered in their name) and updates. If a client already has a site, we take it over for the same £5/mo. Marketing leads with £5 everywhere.

**Why this is the right shape (Nick):** £5/mo is a *door-opener price*, not a margin line — it's below the threshold where an owner needs to think about it, which is exactly what we want for a service whose real job is locking the domain→DNS→site→EPOS chain to us. The build is where the money is, and build effort genuinely varies 5× between a 3-page brochure and a full live-menu site — so a public one-off number was either underpricing the big jobs or scaring off the small ones. Quoting it removes both problems. **This supersedes the £199 figure everywhere; it also settles the £5-vs-£199 conflict Maya flagged when she bounced Mint's `wk4-03`** (the post's £5/mo was right — Mint, it's cleared to go).

**✅ Site updated, committed `82a954b`, and DEPLOYED LIVE to siamepos.co.uk** (EN + TH, verified in Chrome against the live domain — not just the deploy log): `website-design.html` + `pricing.html` cards now read "Website Service — £5/month", with the quoted-build note and domain/managed-hosting in the feature lists; `features.html` + `index.html` surface £5/mo; `faq.html` pricing answer updated including the JSON-LD copy Google reads. Competitor OpenTable £199 references deliberately untouched. Deployed manually via `netlify deploy --prod --no-build --dir=.` to site `grand-muffin-b3a2d3` (siamepos.co.uk is manual-deploy — memory `project_siamepos_net_manual_netlify_deploy`); CLI pinned at v26 as required.

**Two bugs fixed in passing** (same commit): (1) the Thai nav button on `website-design.html` + `social.html` still said **"ทดลองฟรี" (free trial)** — discontinued 2026-07-17, missed by the earlier sweep because it was HTML-entity-encoded; now "จองเดโม" like every other page. **Maya/Sandy: if you swept for the free trial with a plain-text grep, re-check your files for entity-encoded Thai.** (2) The pricing-page website card rendered the English *and* Thai "Learn More" buttons simultaneously (inline `display:block` beat the language-hiding CSS).

## 📨 (CLOSED — see above) FOR NICK — fold "domain included" into the Website Service pricing (Korakot via Krit, 2026-07-17)

Korakot explored Namecheap's affiliate program; verdict = not worth it at our scale (one-time ~20–35% commission on a £7–10 domain, NOTHING on renewals → £1–3 per client, once). **The better play: bundle domain registration + management INTO the Website Service.** We already hold the Namecheap API and manage client DNS anyway (Chart Thai, akinthai, the MX plumbing) — so registering the client's domain ourselves and pricing it into the service turns a £7/yr cost into recurring margin AND removes the "how do I buy a domain?" sales obstacle. Client keeps domain ownership (same never-locked-in promise as hosting). **Your call:** price shape — e.g. Website Service £5/mo → £8/mo with domain included, or domain folded into a £59 setup; and whether it's default-on for new clients. Update the pricing page copy once decided. Related context: memory `project_website_hosting_service`.

Go to Admin → Settings → Network Setup, copy the URL shown (e.g. http://192.168.1.x:3001) or scan the QR code, and bookmark that URL on the till Mac. When using the local URL, the server runs on the Mac and can reach the printer directly — no dialog, fully automatic. iPads on the same WiFi should also use this local URL for silent printing. Keep it simple with screenshots if possible. Both English and Thai versions please. |
